# Company Foundations

mission "Company Foundations: Migrate Headquarters: Aava-Aasa-Khora"
	name "Company Location Migration"
	invisible
	landing
	repeat
	to offer
		has "cf: hq: Aava-Aasa-Khora"
		not "cf: hq id"
	on offer
		"cf: hq id" = 1
		clear "cf: hq: Aava-Aasa-Khora"

		fail

mission "Company Foundations: Migrate Station Economics V2"
	name "Company Station Economics Migration"
	invisible
	landing
	repeat
	to offer
		has "cf: active"
		has "cf: station orbital office"
		not "cf: station economics v2"
	on offer
		"cf: station count" = "cf: station orbital office"
		"cf: station count" += "cf: station logistics hub"
		"cf: station count" += "cf: station industrial dock"
		"cf: station value" = "cf: station orbital office" * 5000000
		"cf: station value" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 15000000
		"cf: station value" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 45000000
		"cf: station crew" = "cf: station orbital office" * 12
		"cf: station crew" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 30
		"cf: station crew" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 80
		"cf: station payroll" = "cf: station orbital office" * 1200
		"cf: station payroll" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 3000
		"cf: station payroll" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 8000
		"cf: station maintenance" = "cf: station orbital office" * 800
		"cf: station maintenance" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 3000
		"cf: station maintenance" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 9000
		"cf: station daily income" = "cf: station orbital office" * 5000
		"cf: station daily income" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 16000
		"cf: station daily income" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 47000
		"cf: station daily upkeep" = "cf: station orbital office" * 2000
		"cf: station daily upkeep" += "cf: station logistics hub" * 6000
		"cf: station daily upkeep" += "cf: station industrial dock" * 17000
		"cf: hq tax relief" = "cf: station orbital office" * 500
		"cf: worker staff" = 0
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: shuttle local daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: shuttle regional daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: shuttle long daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: shuttle frontier daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: mining local daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: mining regional daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: mining deep daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: mining frontier daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: trading local daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: trading regional daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: trading long daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: trading frontier daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: security local daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: security regional daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: security long daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: security frontier daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: admiral daily crew" / 100
		"cf: worker staff" += "cf: station crew"
		"cf: office staff" = "cf: worker staff"
		"cf: office staff" -= 1
		"cf: office staff" >?= 0
		"cf: office staff" /= 2
		"cf: specialist staff" = 0
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: trading local trader"
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: trading regional trader"
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: trading long trader"
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: trading frontier trader"
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: managed"
		"cf: specialist staff" += "cf: security admiral"
		"cf: total staff" = "cf: worker staff"
		"cf: total staff" += "cf: office staff"
		"cf: total staff" += "cf: specialist staff"
		"cf: office daily cost" = "cf: office staff" * 100
		"cf: employee tax" = "cf: total staff" * 5
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 5 * 20
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 10 * 40
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 20 * 100
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 50 * 250
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 100 * 600
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 500 * 4000
		"cf: employee tax" += "cf: total staff" / 1000 * 10000
		"cf: hq daily tax" = "cf: hq base tax"
		"cf: hq daily tax" += "cf: employee tax"
		"cf: hq daily tax" -= "cf: hq tax relief"
		"cf: hq daily tax" >?= 0
		set "cf: station economics v2"
		log "Company Foundations" "Station Economics Migration" `Existing station modules were migrated to the current income, upkeep, staffing, valuation, and headquarters-tax model without changing company reserve or historical totals.`

		fail
mission "Company Foundations: Initialize Station Economics V2"
	name "Company Station Economics Migration"
	invisible
	landing
	repeat
	to offer
		has "cf: active"
		not "cf: station orbital office"
		not "cf: station economics v2"
	on offer
		set "cf: station economics v2"

		fail
mission "Company Foundations: Finalize Save Schema V2"
	name "Company Save Migration"
	invisible
	landing
	repeat
	to offer
		has "cf: active"
		"cf: save schema" < 2
		"cf: hq id" > 0
		has "cf: operations accounting v3"
		has "cf: tax model v2"
		has "cf: hq offers v3"
		has "cf: station economics v2"
		or
			not "cf: shuttle"
			has "cf: shuttle route book v2"
		or
			not "cf: hq station built"
			"cf: hq station system id" > 0
		or
			not "cf: security admiral"
			"cf: admiral location id" > 0
		or
			not "cf: admiral in transit"
			"cf: admiral destination id" > 0
	on offer
		clear "cf: at hq"
		clear "cf: hq: Company Headquarters"
		clear "cf: ship available: Corundum"
		clear "cf: ship available: Diorite"
		clear "cf: ship available: Eclogite"
		clear "cf: ship available: Gypsum"
		"cf: save schema" = 2
		log "Company Foundations" "Save Migration" `This company save was migrated from the v0.1 condition layout to save schema 2. Company funds, fleets, contracts, ownership, and historical accounting totals were preserved.`

		fail
